import compute_all_consistency_scores\\nfrom evaluation import per_instance_f1\\n\\nEXPERIMENTS = {\\n    \\\"exp_002_conll_n8\\\": {\\n        \\\"path\\\": \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/exp002_conll2003/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n        \\\"subtask\\\": \\\"ner\\\",\\n        \\\"dataset\\\": \\\"conll2003\\\",\\n    },\\n    \\\"exp_007_llama_n8\\\": {\\n        \\\"path\\\": \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/exp007_llama_inference/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n        \\\"subtask\\\": \\\"ner\\\",\\n        \\\"dataset\\\": \\\"scierc\\\",\\n    },\\n}\\n\\ndef load_data(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]\\n\\ndef compute_exact_match_rate(samples, subtask):\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        keys = [frozenset((e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in s.get(\\\"entities\\\", [])) for s in samples]\\n    else:\\n        keys = [frozenset((r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in s.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])) for s in samples]\\n    if not keys: return 0.0\\n    c = Counter(keys)\\n    return c.most_common(1)[0][1] / len(samples)\\n\\ndef compute_voting_confidence(samples, subtask):\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    if N == 0: return 0.0\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        for s in samples:\\n            for e in s.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n                counter[(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"])] += 1\\n    else:\\n        for s in samples:\\n            for r in s.get(\\\"relations\\\", []):\\n                counter[(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"])] += 1\\n    if not counter: return 0.0\\n    return float(np.mean([v / N for v in counter.values()]))\\n\\ndef compute_mean_logprob(samples):\\n    lps = [s.get(\\\"mean_logprob\\\") for s in samples if s.get(\\\"mean_logprob\\\") is not None]\\n    lps = [lp for lp in lps if np.isfinite(lp)]\\n    return float(np.mean(lps)) if lps else float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n\\ndef safe_spearman(x, y):\\n    x, y = np.asarray(x, float), np.asarray(y, float)\\n    m = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\\n    x, y = x[m], y[m]\\n    if len(x) < 3: return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    r = spearmanr(x, y)\\n    return float(r.statistic), float(r.pvalue)\\n\\ndef safe_auroc(scores, labels):\\n    scores, labels = np.asarray(scores, float), np.asarray(labels, int)\\n    if len(np.unique(labels)) < 2: return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    n_pos, n_neg = (labels==1).sum(), (labels==0).sum()\\n    if n_pos == 0 or n_neg == 0: return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    ranks = rankdata(scores)\\n    u = ranks[labels==1].sum() - n_pos*(n_pos+1)/2\\n    return float(u / (n_pos * n_neg))\\n\\ndef compute_ece(scores, labels, n_bins=10):\\n    scores, labels = np.asarray(scores, float), np.asarray(labels, float)\\n    mask = np.isfinite(scores)\\n    scores, labels = scores[mask], labels[mask]\\n    if len(scores) == 0: return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    s_min, s_max = scores.min(), scores.max()\\n    if s_max == s_min: return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    norm = (scores - s_min) / (s_max - s_min)\\n    ece = 0.0\\n    for i in range(n_bins):\\n        lo, hi = i / n_bins, (i + 1) / n_bins\\n        mask_bin = (norm >= lo) & (norm < hi) if i < n_bins - 1 else (norm >= lo) & (norm <= hi)\\n        if mask_bin.sum() == 0: continue\\n        avg_conf = norm[mask_bin].mean()\\n        avg_acc = labels[mask_bin].mean()\\n        ece += mask_bin.sum() / len(norm) * abs(avg_conf - avg_acc)\\n    return float(ece)\\n\\ndef analyze(path, subtask):\\n    instances = load_data(path)\\n    entity_key = \\\"entities\\\" if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else \\\"relations\\\"\\n    valid = [inst for inst in instances if len(inst[\\\"gold\\\"].get(entity_key, [])) > 0]\\n\\n    greedy_f1s = []\\n    for inst in valid:\\n        greedy = inst.get(\\\"greedy\\\", inst[\\\"samples\\\"][0])\\n        greedy_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(greedy, inst[\\\"gold\\\"], subtask=subtask))\\n    conditional = [inst for inst, f1 in zip(valid, greedy_f1s) if f1 > 0]\\n\\n    results = {\\\"n_total\\\": len(instances), \\\"n_valid\\\": len(valid), \\\"n_conditional\\\": len(conditional)}\\n    \\n    for split_name, split_insts in [(\\\"full\\\", valid), (\\\"conditional\\\", conditional)]:\\n        cons = compute_all_consistency_scores(split_insts, subtask=subtask)\\n        sj = cons[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"]\\n        fk = cons[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"]\\n        \\n        lp, em, vc, f1s = [], [], [], []\\n        for inst in split_insts:\\n            samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n            greedy = inst.get(\\\"greedy\\\", samples[0])\\n            lp.append(compute_mean_logprob(samples))\\n            em.append(compute_exact_match_rate(samples, subtask))\\n            vc.append(compute_voting_confidence(samples, subtask))\\n            f1s.append(per_instance_f1(greedy, inst[\\\"gold\\\"], subtask=subtask))\\n        \\n        signals = {\\\"SJ\\\": np.array(sj), \\\"FK\\\": np.array(fk), \\\"logprob\\\": np.array(lp),\\n                    \\\"EM\\\": np.array(em), \\\"voting_conf\\\": np.array(vc)}\\n        f1_arr = np.array(f1s)\\n        binary = (f1_arr >= 1.0).astype(int)\\n        \\n        split_res = {\\\"n\\\": len(split_insts)}\\n        for name, vals in signals.items():\\n            rho, p_rho = safe_spearman(vals, f1_arr)\\n            auroc = safe_auroc(vals, binary)\\n            ece = compute_ece(vals, f1_arr)\\n            split_res[name] = {\\\"rho\\\": round(rho, 4), \\\"p_rho\\\": p_rho, \\\"auroc\\\": round(auroc, 4), \\\"ece\\\": round(ece, 4)}\\n        \\n        split_res[\\\"greedy_f1_mean\\\"] = round(float(np.mean(f1s)), 4)\\n        results[split_name] = split_res\\n    return results\\n\\nall_results = {}\\nfor exp_id, cfg in EXPERIMENTS.items():\\n    print(f\\\"Processing {exp_id} ({cfg['dataset']})...\\\")\\n    all_results[exp_id] = analyze(cfg[\\\"path\\\"], cfg[\\\"subtask\\\"])\\n    all_results[exp_id][\\\"dataset\\\"] = cfg[\\\"dataset\\\"]\\n\\nout_path = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/n8_5signal_results.json\\\"\\nwith open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Consistency metrics for structured information extraction with self-consistency.\\n\\nImplements three complementary consistency measures over N sampled extractions:\\n1. Fleiss' Kappa (surface matching) — treats N samples as N raters on entity presence\\n2. Structural soft Jaccard — span-level soft overlap with Hungarian matching\\n3. Oracle best-of-N — upper-bound F1 by selecting the best sample per instance\\n\\nKey dependencies: numpy, scipy (linear_sum_assignment).\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom itertools import combinations\\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment\\n\\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\\n    pass\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Type aliases (mirrors shared data format)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\nEntity = dict       # {\\\"text\\\", \\\"type\\\", \\\"start\\\", \\\"end\\\"}\\nRelation = dict     # {\\\"head\\\", \\\"tail\\\", \\\"type\\\", ...}\\nEvent = dict        # {\\\"trigger\\\": {...}, \\\"arguments\\\": [...]}\\nExtraction = dict   # {\\\"entities\\\", \\\"relations\\\", \\\"events\\\"}\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# 1. Fleiss' Kappa — surface-level entity agreement\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef _extract_surface_keys(sample: Extraction, subtask: str) -> set[tuple]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract surface-level keys from a sample for Fleiss' Kappa computation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n        return {(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in sample.get(\\\"entities\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"re\\\":\\n        return {(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in sample.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])}\\n    elif subtask == \\\"eae\\\":\\n        keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n        for ev in sample.get(\\\"events\\\", []):\\n            t = ev.get(\\\"trigger\\\", {})\\n            for arg in ev.get(\\\"arguments\\\", []):\\n                keys.add((t.get(\\\"text\\\", \\\"\\\"), t.get(\\\"type\\\", \\\"\\\"), arg[\\\"role\\\"], arg[\\\"text\\\"]))\\n        return keys\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown subtask: {subtask}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef fleiss_kappa_surface(samples: list[Extraction], subtask: str = \\\"ner\\\") -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute Fleiss' kappa treating N LLM samples as N raters on item presence.\\n\\n    Items are subtask-dependent surface keys:\\n    - ner: (entity_text, entity_type)\\n    - re: (head_text, tail_text, relation_type)\\n    - eae: (trigger_text, trigger_type, role, argument_text)\\n\\n    Args:\\n        samples: List of N extraction dicts.\\n        subtask: \\\"ner\\\", \\\"re\\\", or \\\"eae\\\".\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Fleiss' kappa in [-1, 1].  Returns 1.0 for degenerate cases (<=1 unique\\n        item, perfect agreement, or undefined P_e).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n_raters = len(samples)\\n    if n_raters <= 1:\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    # Collect unique keys and per-sample presence sets\\n    entity_sets: list[set[tuple]] = []\\n    all_keys: set[tuple] = set()\\n    for sample in samples:\\n        keys = _extract_surface_keys(sample, subtask)\\n        entity_sets.append(keys)\\n        all_keys |= keys\\n\\n    n_subjects = len(all_keys)\\n    if n_subjects <= 0:\\n        # All samples extracted nothing — perfect agreement on empty set\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    key_list = sorted(all_keys)\\n\\n    # Build rating matrix: n_subjects x 2 (categories: absent=0, present=1)\\n    # n_ij = number of raters assigning subject i to category j\\n    rating = np.zeros((n_subjects, 2), dtype=np.int64)\\n    for es in entity_sets:\\n        for idx, key in enumerate(key_list):\\n            if key in es:\\n                rating[idx, 1] += 1\\n            else:\\n                rating[idx, 0] += 1\\n\\n    n = n_raters\\n    k = 2  # number of categories\\n\\n    # Check perfect agreement: every subject has all votes in one category\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

{

Tests whether two-stage cascaded selection\n(filter by signal A top-k, then pick best by signal B) outperforms\nsingle-signal selection.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nsys.path.insert(0, '/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code')\nfrom consistency import (\n    _ner_soft_jaccard_pair,\n    _re_soft_jaccard_pair,\n    _extract_surface_keys,\n)\nfrom evaluation import per_instance_f1\n\nBASE = \"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie\"\n\nDATASETS = {\n    \"scierc_ner\": {\n        \"path\": f\"{BASE}/output/exp_012_rerun_1024/samples.jsonl\",\n        \"subtask\": \"ner\",\n    },\n    \"conll_ner\": {\n        \"path\": f\"{BASE}/output/exp002_conll2003/samples.jsonl\",\n        \"subtask\": \"ner\",\n    },\n    \"scierc_re\": {\n        \"path\": f\"{BASE}/output/exp_012_rerun_1024/samples.jsonl\",\n        \"subtask\": \"re\",\n    },\n}\n\nK_VALUES = [2, 3, 4, 5, 6]\nN_RANDOM = 1000\nRANDOM_SEED = 42\n\n\ndef load_data(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]\n\n\ndef compute_sample_sj_scores(instance, subtask):\n    samples = instance[\"samples\"]\n    N = len(samples)\n    field = \"entities\" if subtask == \"ner\" else \"relations\"\n    pair_fn = _ner_soft_jaccard_pair if subtask == \"ner\" else _re_soft_jaccard_pair\n    matrix = np.zeros((N, N))\n    for i in range(N):\n        for j in range(i + 1, N):\n            s = pair_fn(samples[i].get(field, []), samples[j].get(field, []))\n            matrix[i][j] = s\n            matrix[j][i] = s\n    np.fill_diagonal(matrix, 1.0)\n    return [float(np.mean([matrix[k][j] for j in range(N) if j != k])) for k in range(N)]\n\n\ndef compute_sample_vc_scores(instance, subtask):\n    samples = instance[\"samples\"]\n    N = len(samples)\n    key_sets = [frozenset(_extract_surface_keys(s, subtask)) for s in samples]\n    all_keys = set()\n    for ks in key_sets:\n        all_keys |= ks\n    if not all_keys:\n        return [1.0] * N\n\n    key_counts = Counter()\n    for ks in key_sets:\n        for k in ks:\n            key_counts[k] += 1\n\n    scores = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        if not key_sets[i]:\n            scores.append(0.0)\n        else:\n            avg_vote = np.mean([key_counts[k] / N for k in key_sets[i]])\n            scores.append(float(avg_vote))\n    return scores\n\n\ndef compute_sample_fk_scores(instance, subtask):\n    samples = instance[\"samples\"]\n    N = len(samples)\n    key_sets = [frozenset(_extract_surface_keys(s, subtask)) for s in samples]\n    scores = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        sims = []\n        for j in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                continue\n            union = len(key_sets[i] | key_sets[j])\n            inter = len(key_sets[i] & key_sets[j])\n            sims.append(inter / union if union > 0 else 1.0)\n        scores.append(float(np.mean(sims)) if sims else 1.0)\n    return scores\n\n\ndef compute_sample_em_scores(instance, subtask):\n    samples = instance[\"samples\"]\n    N = len(samples)\n    key_sets = [frozenset(_extract_surface_keys(s, subtask)) for s in samples]\n    counter = Counter(key_sets)\n    return [counter[key_sets[i]] / N for i in range(N)]\n\n\ndef compute_sample_lp_scores(instance):\n    if \"logprobs\" in instance and instance[\"logprobs\"]:\n        return list(instance[\"logprobs\"])\n    return [s.get(\"mean_logprob\", float(\"-inf\")) for s in instance[\"samples\"]]\n\n\ndef cascade_select(scores_a, scores_b, k):\n    N = len(scores_a)\n    k = min(k, N)\n    top_k_idx = np.argsort(scores_a)[-k:]\n    best_idx = top_k_idx[np.argmax([scores_b[i] for i in top_k_idx])]\n    return int(best_idx)\n\n\ndef analyze_dataset(name, config):\n    path = config[\"path\"]\n    subtask = config[\"subtask\"]\n    instances = load_data(path)\n\n    entity_key = \"entities\" if subtask == \"ner\" else \"relations\"\n    valid = [inst for inst in instances if len(inst[\"gold\"].get(entity_key, [])) > 0]\n\n    print(f\"  {name}: {len(instances)} total, {len(valid)} gold-nonempty\")\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(RANDOM_SEED)\n\n    greedy_f1s = []\n    oracle_f1s = []\n    random_f1_matrix = []\n\n    signal_names = [\"LP\", \"VC\", \"SJ\", \"FK\", \"EM\"]\n    single_selected_f1s = {s: [] for s in signal_names}\n\n    cascade_configs = [\n        (\"vc_then_lp\", \"VC\", \"LP\"),\n        (\"lp_then_vc\", \"LP\", \"VC\"),\n        (\"sj_then_lp\", \"SJ\", \"LP\"),\n        (\"sj_then_vc\", \"SJ\", \"VC\"),\n        (\"fk_then_lp\", \"FK\", \"LP\"),\n        (\"em_then_lp\", \"EM\", \"LP\"),\n    ]\n    cascade_f1s = {f\"{c[0]}_k{k}\": [] for c in cascade_configs for k in K_VALUES}\n\n    for inst in valid:\n        samples = inst[\"samples\"]\n        N = len(samples)\n        gold = inst[\"gold\"]\n\n        sample_f1s = [per_instance_f1(s, gold, subtask=subtask) for s in samples]\n\n        greedy = inst.get(\"greedy\", samples[0])\n        greedy_f1 = per_instance_f1(greedy, gold, subtask=subtask)\n        greedy_f1s.append(greedy_f1)\n\n        oracle_f1s.append(max(sample_f1s))\n\n        rand_f1s = [sample_f1s[rng.randint(N)] for _ in range(N_RANDOM)]\n        random_f1_matrix.append(rand_f1s)\n\n        scores = {\n            \"LP\": compute_sample_lp_scores(inst),\n            \"VC\": compute_sample_vc_scores(inst, subtask),\n            \"SJ\": compute_sample_sj_scores(inst, subtask),\n            \"FK\": compute_sample_fk_scores(inst, subtask),\n            \"EM\": compute_sample_em_scores(inst, subtask),\n        }\n\n        for sig in signal_names:\n            best_idx = int(np.argmax(scores[sig]))\n            single_selected_f1s[sig].append(sample_f1s[best_idx])\n\n        for cascade_name, sig_a, sig_b in cascade_configs:\n            for k in K_VALUES:\n                idx = cascade_select(scores[sig_a], scores[sig_b], k)\n                cascade_f1s[f\"{cascade_name}_k{k}\"].append(sample_f1s[idx])\n\n    greedy_f1 = float(np.mean(greedy_f1s))\n    oracle_f1 = float(np.mean(oracle_f1s))\n    random_f1 = float(np.mean([np.mean(r) for r in random_f1_matrix]))\n\n    result = {\n        \"n_instances\": len(valid),\n        \"greedy_f1\": round(greedy_f1, 4),\n        \"oracle_f1\": round(oracle_f1, 4),\n        \"random_f1\": round(random_f1, 4),\n    }\n\n    for sig in signal_names:\n        result[f\"{sig.lower()}_only_f1\"] = round(float(np.mean(single_selected_f1s[sig])), 4)\n\n    for cascade_name, sig_a, sig_b in cascade_configs:\n        cascade_result = {}\n        for k in K_VALUES:\n            key = f\"{cascade_name}_k{k}\"\n            f1_val = round(float(np.mean(cascade_f1s[key])), 4)\n            cascade_result[f\"k{k}\"] = f1_val\n        result[cascade_name] = cascade_result\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef find_best_cascade(results):\n    best_config = None\n    best_improvement = -999\n    best_f1 = -999\n\n    for ds_name, ds_res in results.items():\n        if ds_name in (\"best_cascade_config\", \"conclusion\"):\n            continue\n        single_best_sig = None\n        single_best_f1 = -1\n        for sig in [\"lp\", \"vc\", \"sj\", \"fk\", \"em\"]:\n            f1 = ds_res.get(f\"{sig}_only_f1\", 0)\n            if f1 > single_best_f1:\n                single_best_f1 = f1\n                single_best_sig = sig\n\n        for cascade_name in [\"vc_then_lp\", \"lp_then_vc\", \"sj_then_lp\", \"sj_then_vc\", \"fk_then_lp\", \"em_then_lp\"]:\n            if cascade_name not in ds_res:\n                continue\n            for k_str, f1 in ds_res[cascade_name].items():\n                improvement = f1 - single_best_f1\n                if improvement > best_improvement:\n                    best_improvement = improvement\n                    best_f1 = f1\n                    best_config = f\"{ds_name}/{cascade_name}/{k_str} (f1={f1:.4f}, Δ={improvement:+.4f} vs {single_best_sig}_only={single_best_f1:.4f})\"\n\n    return best_config, best_improvement\n\n\ndef main():\n    all_results = {}\n\n    for name, config in DATASETS.items():\n        print(f\"\\nAnalyzing {name}...\")\n        all_results[name] = analyze_dataset(name, config)\n\n    best_config, best_improvement = find_best_cascade(all_results)\n    all_results[\"best_cascade_config\"] = best_config\n\n    any_improvement = best_improvement >= 0.01\n    if any_improvement:\n        all_results[\"conclusion\"] = f\"Cascade selection improves over single-signal by up to {best_improvement:+.4f} F1. Best: {best_config}\"\n    else:\n        all_results[\"conclusion\"] = f\"Cascade selection does NOT consistently improve over single-signal selection. Max improvement: {best_improvement:+.4f} F1.\"\n\n    out_path = f\"{BASE}/output/review_round9_experiments/vc_lp_cascade/cascade_results.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSaved: {out_path}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    for ds_name in [\"scierc_ner\", \"conll_ner\", \"scierc_re\"]:\n        if ds_name not in all_results:\n            continue\n        r = all_results[ds_name]\n        print(f\"\\n--- {ds_name} ({r['n_instances']} instances) ---\")\n        print(f\"  Greedy:  {r['greedy_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  Random:  {r['random_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  Oracle:  {r['oracle_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  LP-only: {r['lp_only_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  VC-only: {r['vc_only_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  SJ-only: {r['sj_only_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  FK-only: {r['fk_only_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  EM-only: {r['em_only_f1']:.4f}\")\n        print()\n\n        for cascade_name in [\"vc_then_lp\", \"lp_then_vc\", \"sj_then_lp\", \"sj_then_vc\", \"fk_then_lp\", \"em_then_lp\"]:\n            if cascade_name not in r:\n                continue\n            vals = r[cascade_name]\n            k_str = \" | \".join([f\"k{k}={vals[f'k{k}']:.4f}\" for k in K_VALUES])\n            print(f\"  {cascade_name}: {k_str}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nBest cascade: {all_results['best_cascade_config']}\")\n    print(f\"Conclusion: {all_results['conclusion']}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF",
  "account": "root"
}
